&-\frac{1}{64}W^{-1}\varepsilon{}_{i j}(\Gamma{}^{a}){}_{\alpha}{}^{\beta}\varepsilon{}^{j k}w{}^{\alpha \gamma}w{}_{\beta \gamma}\delta{}^{i}{}_{k}\big(\nabla_{a}X{}^{l}{}_{l}\big) -\frac{1}{256}W^{-1}\varepsilon{}_{i j}(\Gamma{}^{a}){}^{\alpha}{}_{\alpha}\varepsilon{}^{j k}w{}^{\beta \gamma}w{}_{\beta \gamma}\delta{}^{i}{}_{k}\big(\nabla_{a}X{}^{l}{}_{l}\big)\\

&+\frac{1}{96}W^{-1}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\left.\mathcal R(J){}^{a}{}_{\alpha k}{}^{l k}\right|\varepsilon{}^{i j}\big(\nabla_{a}w{}^{\alpha}{}_{\beta}\big)\lambda{}^{\beta}{}_{l} +\frac{1}{96}W^{-1}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\left.\mathcal R(J){}^{a}{}_{\alpha k}{}^{l k}\right|\varepsilon{}^{i j}w{}^{\alpha}{}_{\beta}\big(\nabla_{a}\lambda{}^{\beta}{}_{l}\big)\\
&-\frac{1}{6}W^{-1}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\left.\mathcal R(J){}^{a}{}_{\alpha k}{}^{l k}\right|\varepsilon{}^{i j}\chi{}^{\alpha}{}_{l}\big(\nabla_{a}W\big) +\frac{1}{48}W^{-1}\left.\mathcal R(J){}^{a}{}_{\alpha i}{}^{j i}\right|(\Gamma{}^{b}){}^{\alpha \beta}\big(\nabla_{a}\nabla_{b}\lambda{}_{\beta j}\big)\\
&-\frac{1}{96}W^{-1}\left.\mathcal R(J){}^{a}{}_{\alpha i}{}^{j i}\right|\big(\nabla_{a}w{}^{\alpha}{}_{\beta}\big)\lambda{}^{\beta}{}_{j} -\frac{1}{96}W^{-1}\left.\mathcal R(J){}^{a}{}_{\alpha i}{}^{j i}\right|w{}^{\alpha}{}_{\beta}\big(\nabla_{a}\lambda{}^{\beta}{}_{j}\big)\\

&+\frac{1}{96}W^{-1}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\left.\mathcal R(J){}^{a}{}_{\alpha k}{}^{l k}\right|\left.\mathcal T{}_{a}{}^{\alpha \beta}\right|\varepsilon{}^{i j}w{}_{\beta \gamma}\lambda{}^{\gamma}{}_{l} +\frac{1}{48}W^{-1}\left.\mathcal R(J){}^{a}{}_{\alpha i}{}^{j i}\right|\left.\mathcal T{}_{a}{}^{\alpha \beta}\right|(\Gamma{}^{b}){}_{\beta}{}^{\gamma}\big(\nabla_{b}\lambda{}_{\gamma j}\big)\\
&-\frac{1}{96}W^{-1}\left.\mathcal R(J){}^{a}{}_{\alpha i}{}^{j i}\right|\left.\mathcal T{}_{a}{}^{\alpha \beta}\right|w{}_{\beta \gamma}\lambda{}^{\gamma}{}_{j} +\frac{i}{24}W^{-1}\left.\mathcal R(J){}^{a}{}_{\alpha i}{}^{j i}\right|\left.\mathcal R(\mathbb D){}_{a}{}^{\alpha k}\right|X{}_{k j}
